# HANDOFF - Prime-Toeplitz / R-INT program: lead-agent onboarding

Rewritten 2026-06-14 in the disciplined reset (see doc 129). Read this
first; then doc 45 (the spec); then the no-go bank (docs 51-70 + the
survivors listed below); then the docs 00-44 arc as needed.

## 1. What this is

An unconditional-RH research program. The original arc (docs 00-16)
produced the TRACKING EQUIVALENCE (RH <=> uniform tau < 1; doc 13,
preprint research/preprint/tracking-equivalence-RH.tex) and a no-go
map. The current arc (docs 17-40) reconstructed the SUPPLY SIDE of the
completed zeta function from integer cardinalities (THH count ladders,
no zeta input), realized a compactified local object as a spectrum, and
reduced everything to ONE missing object with a complete specification.
Second preprint: research/preprint/count-ladders-index-boundary.tex.

The operating mode is fixed: **numerics + no-go theorems + sharp narrowing**.
Every cycle produces a certified numerical witness OR a no-go theorem
with a named mechanism. Nothing else counts as progress (doc 129).

## 3. The 2026-06-14 reset (read doc 129)

The deleted "candidate-driven assault" / construction-naming era
and the 71-128 process bloat (ledgers, routers, admission matrices,
status boards, dispatch/expert packets, rubrics) were DELETED. That era
produced zero theorems and zero witnesses across 68 documents while a
single target name grew one qualifier per doc - a degrees-of-freedom
violation (G-INT6): zero matched constraints against unbounded naming
choices. Doc 129 is the autopsy and standing no-go; it is the only doc
permitted to name the dead lineage. The deletion is recoverable in git
history at the checkpoint commit preceding the reset.

## 3a. Post-reset cycles 1-5 + consolidation (read doc 136 FIRST for state)

Five disciplined no-go cycles (2026-06-14..16) then closed the analytic
frontier, each shipping a no-go theorem with a named mechanism and
lead-verified witnesses:

  130  second-evaluation no-go (no unforced odd slot without properness);
  132  radius-circularity (the proper object can't fix its own radius:
       Euler EDGE free, even SPLIT = RH);
  133  the radius is irreducibly global (local route automorphy-firewalled;
       banked global suppliers + both flows FE-blind);
  134  the Hilbert-Polya/reality route reduces to the radius wall
       (positivity-by-axiom imports RH; forcing needs the missing Rosati);
  135  PHASE-CERT collapses (arg m positivity = the doc-13 zero
       cross-correlation = Weil positivity, RH-equivalent).

DOC 136 IS THE CONSOLIDATION MAP and the current state-of-program: every
banked analytic route (local/global, modulus/phase) factors through ONE
missing object -- End(S^2/Z) with a positive Rosati involution
F^dag = (deg) F^{-1} (the CC C x C / scaling-site Jacobian, present over
F_q, absent over Z = the F_1/Connes/Deninger object). BOTH analytic faces
(modulus 132/133, phase 134/135) are RH-equivalent; there is no analytic
shortcut. This is STRATEGY sec-4's win-win NEGATIVE branch, executed. The
sole remaining target is BUILDING End(S^2/Z) (a famous decades-open
construction -- a long arc, not a quick cycle); do not re-attack the closed
analytic faces (standing kill-ledger, doc 136 sec 7). Honest scope: these
are relocations + characterizations at the program's float-witness rigor,
not unconditional impossibility theorems (doc 136 sec 6).

## 3b. The construction arc is OPEN (doc 137 charter)

As of 2026-06-17 the End(S^2/Z) construction arc is open; DOC 137 IS ITS
CHARTER. The object has two layers: LAYER I the stage (the FE/symmetry
object Xbar -- already realized, pi_*=G mod the banked Thm S.1, doc 25/30;
DD.4 Lin clause proven EE.1) and LAYER II the play (the positive Rosati
involution F^dag=(deg)F^{-1} whose positivity <=> RH -- the RH-grade target
no analytic route supplies, doc 136). Risk-ladder: R0 foundation (done:
S.1, Xbar) -> R1 DD.4 weights>=1 (THE ENTRY, building on EE.1) -> R2 the
archimedean unit -> R3 the positive Rosati (Layer II). Three candidate
routes (doc 137 sec 6): in-house PFD/Xbar (default), Connes-Consani
scaling-site / C x C, condensed-metric enrichment. BINDING: this arc is the
exact shape that went off the rails before, so it runs under the strictest
doc-129 anti-regress contract (doc 137 sec 7) -- every cycle a verified
sub-object or a no-go; no qualifier-accretion; stage results never promoted
(BB.1). R1 has two hundred ten banked no-go cycles plus three hundred six construction-spec

## 4. How to navigate the record

research/proof/00-16: the tracking arc. 17-40: the R-INT arc (each doc
a cycle synthesis; CORRECTIONS ARE IN PLACE - docs 18/20/23/24/25 carry
dated correction sections; never read an early claim without its
corrections). 41-50: the spec consolidation + construction blueprint +
external packets. 51-70 + survivors: the no-go bank (sec 2). 129: the
reset autopsy. research/proof/TODO.md: the risk ladder. README.md: the
per-doc index. research/work/[G..AC]-*.md: worker records.
scripts/numerics/witness/: ~200 fail-closed witnesses, run via
  python3 -m scripts.numerics.witness.<name>
(exit 0 = the recorded claim reproduces; stack: python-flint, numpy;
tectonic for preprints; NO pdflatex).

## 5. The operating system (how the program is run)

LEAD ROLE: design cycles, dispatch FRESH-CONTEXT background workers
(3-5 per cycle), verify every landing (rerun the witness yourself
before committing), apply errata across docs, write the cycle
synthesis, keep TODO + the memory file current, commit atomically with
the Co-Authored-By trailer (guard.sh runs per commit). Workers NEVER
commit; they write to research/work/<ID>-*.md + witnesses only.

STANDING DISCIPLINE (non-negotiable):
- Every cycle ships a certified witness OR a no-go theorem with a named
  mechanism (doc 129). A schema with no witness is not a document.
- Pre-registration before computation; refuted registrations are
  recorded, never relocated.
- Fail-closed: negatives are full-value; every kill names its mechanism.
- Witnesses: exit-gated, deterministic, rerun by the lead at landing.
- Certified, not floating: every numeric claim is a python-flint ball
  enclosure; every structural claim is exact linear algebra (STRATEGY
  sec 8).
- FREEZE PROTOCOL for any zero contact (loci computed and hashed BEFORE
  zeta_gammas is imported; comparison only).
- Fork protocol (doc 14): F1 value fork, F2 deformation kill, P1 extra
  information, P2 carrier non-existence, P3 second-evaluation
  independence.
- Gates G-INT1-6 (esp. G-INT6 DOF ledger: #constraints must exceed
  #choices or it is calibration) and G-CH1-4 (doc 38).
- Carrier guards: every construction must die on DH (no carrier) and
  Epstein (dies at multiplicativity/effectivity/address - exhibit the
  exact death point); use doc 64 instead of re-running Epstein.
- Claims grading everywhere: Theorem / Certified / Census / Reading.

## 6. What NOT to do

- Do not chase a concrete construction in hope of stumbling onto the
  missing object (doc 129 / STRATEGY sec 6). Construction re-enters only
  against one of the two genuine frontiers (sec 2), and only with a
  runnable witness. Do not grow a target name by qualifier accretion;
  a needed new qualifier is a signal to STOP and run a witness or prove
  the impossibility (Cor 129.2).
- Do not insert the unit (zero-slack is a theorem: LL/NN - it must be
  derived; any insertion is logged and demotes the result).
- Do not trust shape data as object data (AB's split).
- Do not read zero data before freezing.
- Do not claim RH progress from FE-layer results (BB.1 is the guard).
- Do not prove more RH-equivalent reformulations - they hold for DH
  (STRATEGY sec 6).
The user sets strategy at decision points; execute autonomously between
them and report outcome-first.
